The work spanned: reading the handoff and skill files → writing the script → writing image-generation prompts → assembling VO → exporting Figma frames → building the Remotion video → many rounds of user-directed refinement on timing, lip sync, captions, gaps, and SFX.

Standing instruction from the user: *"make sure that even if your context window shrinks, you don't forget the gist of this entire video/chat/conversation so that I don't start feeding you same data and info again for the nth time."* → satisfied by `~/.claude/projects/-Users-sejal/memory/project-ep003-remote-control.md`.

2. **Key Technical Concepts:**
- **Remotion 4** composition rendering (9:16, 1080x1920, 30fps)
- **Data-driven cut**: all timings in `src/rc_cut.json`, emitted by a Python script; nothing typed into TSX
- **Whisper** (small model) for word-level transcription; **RMS envelope** analysis for true audio edges (skill rule: *word ORDER from whisper, word EDGES from the signal*)
- **ffmpeg**: `-ss` BEFORE `-i` (else afade silences the clip); `atempo` for speed; `afade` 10–20ms at cuts
- **Lip flap**: swap between REAL drawn frames only, driven by VO envelope — never paint mouths
- **AI mark**: six petals (`cx 50 cy 25 rx 14.5 ry 25`, rotated i×60°), white eye ring r17, teal pupil r11, `gaze=1` so eye sits right of centre at x=59, viewBox `-6 -6 112 112`, colour `#03A6CF`
- **Caption style (series spec)**: Lato weight **800**, size 32 in 720-space (48 at 1080), line-height 42/32, **white fill + 5px (720-space) black stroke**, `paint-order: stroke`
- **SFX lettering**: snap in ~0.05s, overshoot, decay over 0.26s, hold, clear — never baked flat
- **Word-anchored line addressing**: seed on a unique word, grow phrase from measured envelope runs
- **Figma MCP** `use_figma` (hide/restore nodes) + `download_assets` (export at scale 1.5)

3. **Files and Code Sections:**

**`~/Documents/comics-video/v10/rc_audio.py`** (was `/tmp/build_final.py`) — builds her VO track.
Key structure: segments concatenated in order `["M2a","g1","M2b","Sb","M3a","g2","M3b","S11","Sp","T1","Sc","T2","Sq","T3a","gq","T3p","gp","T3b"]`. TEMPO=1.15, K=1.4/1.15. Notable:
```python
# Her line is CUT OFF by the ping, mid-word on "non-refun-". The cut is the
# point, so it gets a 20ms fade rather than a clean ending (comic-audio.md).
run(["ffmpeg","-v","error","-y","-ss","17.80","-t","%.3f"%(22.71-17.80),"-i",M,...
     "-af","afade=t=in:st=0:d=0.010,afade=t=out:st=%.3f:d=0.020"%((22.71-17.80)-0.020),T+"/M3b.wav"])
```
Gaps: `Sb` (his bluff), `g1`=4.25 (beat after "On it."), `Sq`=2.70, `gp`=0.90.

**`~/Documents/comics-video/v10/rc_plates.py`** — builds every plate from raw Figma exports. Masks dialogue captions (`fill_flat`), then builds mouth variants **decided by ink measurement, never assumption**:
```python
def ink(n, box):
    y0,y1,x0,x1 = box
    return int((L(n)[y0:y1, x0:x1] < 120).sum())
def pair(a, b, box):
    return (a,b) if ink(a,box) > ink(b,box) else (b,a)
HER2=(867,903,249,309); HER3=(878,905,255,306)
HIM_2S=(848,880,767,823); HIM_CU=(1348,1425,398,572); HIM_89=(1103,1157,382,499)
build(["1","3","7","10","14","15","16","17"], HER2,  "scene1","scene2")
build(["22","23","24"],                        HER3,  "scene22","scene23")
build(["8","9"],                               HIM_89,"scene8","scene9")
build(["20","21"],                             HIM_CU,"scene20","scene21")
```
Result: **her two-shot open ← scene2** (NOT scene1), him two-shot open ← scene11. Also builds `clean11_{oo,os,so,ss}` four-way for the two-shot where both mouths move.

**`~/Documents/comics-video/v10/rc_emit.py`** — writes `src/rc_cut.json`. Word-anchored line addressing:
```python
SEEDS = [("next","NEXT"),("guessed","GUESS"),("nine","PAID"),("talking","WHO"),
         ("try","TRY"),("hold","HOLD"),("raising","PR"),("appetiz","REMOTE"),
         ("pings","PINGS"),("idiot","IDIOT")]
def _seed(key):
    for t,a,b in _words:
        if key in t: return (a+b)/2
    raise SystemExit("word %r is not in her VO -- check the take" % key)
```
Runs join their seed, then grow outward across gaps < 0.30s. A run containing two seeds is split at their midpoint.

Lip flap (local-adaptive, the final correct version):
```python
def flap(ids):
    """Open near each syllable peak, shut in the dip between, shut on silence.
    The reference is LOCAL (+/-0.35s) -- a global threshold either holds the
    mouth open through a loud line or never opens it on a quiet one."""
    ...
    FLOOR=np.median(sp)-24; Rw=int(0.35/HOP); st=False
    for i,v in enumerate(lvl):
        if v<FLOOR: st=False
        else:
            peak=lvl[max(0,i-Rw):min(len(lvl),i+Rw+1)].max()
            if not st and v>peak-7.0: st=True
            elif st and v<peak-13.0: st=False
```
Plus overrides: `fm=hold(fm, C["uh"], C["pingH"])` (he holds "uh" till the ping); scene14 forced open through ping2 and shut on ping3.

     dict(id="ping3", t=C["ping3"], x=802, y=1350, col=W),
     dict(id="ping4", t=C["ping4"], x=726, y=1294, col=W),

**`~/Documents/comics-video/src/RemoteControl.tsx`** — the composition. Key parts:
```tsx
const CAP = "'LatoLocal','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif";
const MONO = "'GeistMono','SFMono-Regular',monospace";
const S = 1.5;
// caption: Lato 800, white fill, 5px/720 black stroke, paintOrder stroke
const Caption = ({c}) => (<div style={{... fontFamily: CAP, fontSize: 32*S,
  fontWeight: 800, lineHeight: 42/32, color:"#FFFFFF",
  WebkitTextStroke: `${5*S}px #000000`, paintOrder:"stroke"}}>{c.text}</div>);

const SfxWord: React.FC<{s; t}> = ({s, t}) => {
  const e = t - s.t;
  if (e < -0.02 || e > 1.15) return null;
  const snap = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, e / 0.05));
  const decay = e <= 0.05 ? 0 : Math.min(1, (e - 0.05) / 0.26);
  const scale = 1 + 0.26 * snap * (1 - decay);
  const hold = e > 0.78 ? Math.max(0, 1 - (e - 0.78) / 0.28) : 1;
  ... fontSize: 25*S, fontWeight: 800, fontStyle:"italic", color: s.col
};
```
Plate selection handles `"both"` (four-way) and `"her"/"him"` (open/shut). `date_main` MUST be wrapped in `<Sequence from={c.frame}>` with the volume callback adding `dateStart` back. "AT THE OFFICE" label in Geist Mono above the monitor on split frames.

**`~/Documents/comics-video/src/AIMark.tsx`** — instant eye pop (no easing):
```tsx
// The eye POPS open -- no easing. A lid that slides up reads as a UI
// animation; a hard switch reads as something waking up.
const lid = asleep ? 1 : 0;
const lidR = asleep ? 0 : 11;
const pop = 1 + (asleep ? 0 : Math.max(0, 1 - Math.max(0, since) / 0.16) * 0.13);
```

4. **Errors and fixes:**

- **Claimed Figma was unauthenticated** — user: *"is figma not connected?"* I tested `whoami`, it WAS authenticated. Only `plugin:figma:figma` and `mobbin` need auth.
- **Letterboxed plates** — my prompt wording *"the TOP THIRD of the frame is empty pale background"* read as "leave blank canvas." Fix: **FULL BLEED** wording + *"empty of objects, not empty of artwork."*
- **`date_main` not in a `<Sequence>`** — played from frame 0; every gap built for Peter landed wrong.
- **16 kHz resample destroyed her VO** — user: *"fan fucking tastic now the VO of the date is completely gone."* Fix: rebuild from source at 44.1 kHz; **analyse at any rate, APPLY at native**.
- **Over-eager sigh detector** ate consonants (removed 1.13s / 9 "sighs"). Fix: conservative pitch-based detector; found only 3 real candidates, all ≤0.14s.
- **Mouth open/shut plates SWAPPED** — user: *"her moouth is supposed to be closed when she crosses her arms ... this is my 6th time telling you to refer the figma frames ... you goldfish."* Measured ink: **scene2 = open, scene1 = closed**. I had it backwards. Fix: `pair()` decides by ink count.
- **Panel order broken** — moved scene3 to slot 9; phone teleported from his hand back to the table. Fix: strict Figma order 1→28.
- **Wrong caption font** — used Poppins 400 black. User: *"did you not read the skill file for the captions?"* Fix: Lato 800 white + 5px black stroke.
- **SFX baked flat** — user: *"Did you not read the skill files on how SFX captions are supposed to be animated?"* Fix: hid the Figma text nodes, re-exported, restored, now rendered live with the pop.
- **Inpainting SFX failed** (smeared line art / dark smudges) → replaced by the Figma hide-export-restore pipeline.
- **`scene19` flapped with no mouth pair** → plate 404 → blank frame. User: *"try me frame is gone."* Fix: guard that drops the flap when plates are missing.
- **Index fragility (twice)** — lines addressed by run index, then by group structure; both broke when a breath was removed or a take replaced. Fix: word anchoring.
- **Duck gap spike at 5.11s** — user spotted it. Two duck windows 0.36s apart let her level pop back to full. Fix: merge windows closer than 0.55s.
- **Beat frame shrank** — audio gap was unchanged (1.43s) but her line grew, so the "..." frame only got 0.70s of silence. Fix: derive tail cuts from measured line ends.
- **Lip sync too twitchy / too phrase-y** — user told me to look at graph. Graph's numbers: 3.4 changes/sec, ~66% open, median run 0.32s. Final fix: local-adaptive threshold.

5. **Problem Solving:**
Solved: VO splicing across 7 delivered takes; ducking architecture (derived from cues, never typed); word-anchored timing that survives take replacement; ink-measured mouth pairs; Figma hide-export-restore for SFX; instant AI eye pop; full audit script run on every build (panel lengths, caption bleeds/overlaps, duck gaps, cue audibility, plate existence, ping-word/sound sync).

## ASSISTANT
**52.0s.**

## She keeps talking

The problem was that the new take replaced a whole block and I put **4.25s of dead silence** where the old take had her rambling. So she only spoke when the script needed her to.

Now the new take stops after *"doesn't ask anyone"* and the **old take picks straight up** — *"doesn't say a word, just drops the confirmation in the family group chat at one in the morning like it's completely normal"* — running unbroken into *"and you know what she did next??"*. One segment, no seam, she never hands him the floor.

I cut the new *"The 1AM confirmation"* line because the old take says the same beat two seconds later and you'd have heard it twice.

She's now voiced **81% / 94% / 69%** under *"oh shit"*, *"Open the PR"* and *"On it."* — she talks through all three. They duck her, but I floored any duck that lands on the rambling at **0.20** instead of letting the stack merge down to 0.13; she's under him, not gone.

One thing that could have bitten: her lines are word-anchored, and a six-second ramble with no seed of its own gets handed to the nearest neighbour — **NEXT** — which would have started *"and you know what she did next??"* six seconds early. It has its own seed now (`normal` → RAMBLE), and the split is clean: ramble **3.23→9.51**, NEXT **9.69→11.23**.

## The "uh" is in both places

| | at | on |
|---|---|---|
| uh | 33.00 | scene 20, the push-in |
| uh | 48.74 | scene 27, head of the last line |

Same file, played twice. The caption reads **"uh… what did your sister do next?"** and starts on the *uh*, and panel 27 now cuts in on it rather than on the line — so the whole thing sits on his face, the way the take originally had it.

**Your comedic gap is intact** — the beat after *"which idiot sits there approving every single step?"* is still 1.5s of silence; I pushed the line later rather than eating into it.

## Separately

The background caption *"she just paid for all nine of us, upfront, non-refundable"* isn't rendering. It's pinned to scene 11, which is only 1.13s and already carries his *"right."* — after clamping and the half-length rule it's under the 0.32s floor and gets dropped. This predates today's changes. Fix is to let it run across scenes 11→12 instead of clamping to one panel; say the word and I'll do it.

Peter's voice still jumps to Eric on *"Run the tests"* and *"it's kind of complicated"*, and the scene4b transition frame isn't generated yet.

**54.4s.** Found it — `scene4.5`, node `647:551`, sitting right of scene4. Exported at 1.5×, cropped from 1082 to 1080 (the base rect starts at x=1 in that frame, so it renders two pixels wide).

## The bridge

**scene4 → scene4.5 → scene5.** Same shot, head up → head tipped down to the phone on the table → then the split screen with the phone in his hand. The jump you didn't like is gone: he doesn't teleport into the office, he looks down first.

**Held 1.80s**, which is the third-longest panel in the film. Nothing happens on it on purpose.

I kept the **"…" you drew** rather than masking it and drawing it live. I tried live first — at the series caption weight (Lato 800, 5px black stroke) three small dots come out as three rings. It carries no VO and nothing animates on it, so baked is correct here.

## Where the time came from

This was the real problem. `p04` and `onit` were hardcoded at 6.15 and 8.15, and her *"and you know what she did next??"* is fixed in her track — there were only **0.9s** of slack. Taking 1.8s would have put the AI's *"On it."* on top of her line.

So I split her ramble at the breath she already takes after *"doesn't say a word,"* and widened it to 2.35s. That silence lands **under Peter's line**, not under the beat:

| | her voice |
|---|---|
| under *"oh shit. my changes are uncommitted."* | 37% |
| beat frame, first 0.7s | **0% — the beat** |
| beat frame, rest | 82% |
| under *"Open the PR for the API fix."* | 93% |
| under *"On it."* | 72% |

She opens the beat in silence, so the hold on him reads, then she's rambling again before the cut to the office — and straight through both office lines. Not a single word of hers was cut.

`P04` and `onit` are now **derived** from the beat instead of typed, so if you want the hold longer or shorter it's one number (`BEAT` in [rc_emit.py](v10/rc_emit.py)) and everything behind it moves.

Audit clean: 26 panels, no caption bleeding its panel, no overlaps, no plate missing, every ping word within 0.00s of its sound, both "uh"s still in place at 35.35 and 51.09.

## ASSISTANT
**54.2s.** Three fixes.

## "On it." was on the monitor

Yes, that's the rule, and I'd inherited Figma's own box for scene6 instead of applying it. Measured the plate: the split screen's only clear area is **y 722–947**, the band between the desk line and the divider. Scene6's caption was at 611–674 — straight across the stand.

Scenes **5, 6, 12 and 13 are all one shot**, so they now share one position, `SPLIT=(540,862)`. That also fixes something you hadn't flagged: *"Permission to push?"* was left-aligned and *"Run the tests."* centred 23px lower, so the caption jumped between two panels of the same shot. All four sit at the same height now.

I added a check to the emitter that measures ink under every caption box and reports any that land on drawn art. It runs on every build — **0 of 19** sitting on ink.

## The gaps were in the wrong place

You're right, and it was my doing. I'd split her rant at *"doesn't say a word,"* and dropped 2.35s in there — a pause in the middle of her own sentence, which is the one place it doesn't belong.

Moved it. She now runs **unbroken** from *"doesn't say a word"* through *"drops the confirmation in the family group chat at one in the morning like it's completely normal"* — longest silence inside that is **0.28s**, a breath. The pause comes after it, and *"and you know what she did next??"* arrives on its own.

Which also puts her voice where it should be:

| | her voice |
|---|---|
| under *"oh shit"* | 81% |
| the beat frame | 93% |
| under *"Open the PR"* | 64% |
| under *"On it."* | 0% |
| before her punchline | 0% |

She talks straight through him and the beat frame, then the AI's line is clean and her punchline is isolated.

## The punchline

It was a **splice artefact**. `peter_uh` already carries 0.14s of tail and `peter_p20` 0.08s of head — 0.22s, which is the take's own hesitation. I'd added another 0.18 on top, making a 0.40s hole that doesn't sound like one breath.

Butted with no gap now. The end reads: her line ends 49.28 → **1.92s** comedic silence (untouched, the one you signed off) → *"uh"* → **0.20s** → *"what did your sister do next?"*

If it still feels long, the thing to shorten is that 1.92s, since the "uh" adds 0.9s that wasn't there when you approved it — say the word and I'll pull it to ~1.4s.
